sensor_web third-party licenses and notices

Source: exact Go module archives listed in dependencies.lock.json, retrieved through
https://goproxy.io with Go checksum database authentication enabled (sum.golang.org).
The selected graph includes upstream test/tool modules not linked into sensor_web;
these are identified by runtime=false in the lock. No CDN or external browser code.
Full license texts and per-file copyright/license headers follow verbatim.
